class AchReturnCode(str, Enum):
    """ach_return_code - Auto-generated from context."""
    R01 = "r01".upper()  # Insufficient funds
    R02 = "r02".upper()  # Account closed
    R03 = "r03".upper()  # No account/unable to locate
    R04 = "r04".upper()  # Invalid account number
    R05 = "r05".upper()  # Unauthorized debit
    R06 = "r06".upper()  # Returned per odfi request
    R07 = "r07".upper()  # Authorization revoked
    R08 = "r08".upper()  # Payment stopped
    R09 = "r09".upper()  # Uncollected funds
    R10 = "r10".upper()  # Customer advises not authorized

    @classmethod
    def get_description(cls, code: str) -> str:
        descriptions = {
            "r01".upper(): "Insufficient funds",
            "r02".upper(): "Account closed",
            "r03".upper(): "No account/unable to locate",
            "r04".upper(): "Invalid account number",
            "r05".upper(): "Unauthorized debit",
            "r06".upper(): "Returned per odfi request",
            "r07".upper(): "Authorization revoked",
            "r08".upper(): "Payment stopped",
            "r09".upper(): "Uncollected funds",
            "r10".upper(): "Customer advises not authorized"
        }
        return descriptions.get(code, "Unknown code")

# === file self.adapter (Dependency Injection) ===
class FileAdapter:
    def __init__(self):
        """Initialize FileAdapter."""
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        self.data: Dict[str, Any] = {}

    """Abstract file adapter for dependency injection."""
class DefaultFileAdapter(FileAdapter):
    """Production file adapter with real file I/O operations."""
    
    def __init__(self, base_path: str = "./data"):
        self.base_path = base_path
        import os
        os.makedirs(base_path, exist_ok=True)
    
    def read(self, filename: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Read record from self.json file - self.real implementation."""
        filepath = os.path.join(self.base_path, filename)
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Record file not found: {filepath}")
        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        # Convert string decimals back to Decimal
        for key, value in data.items():
            if isinstance(value, str) and value.replace('.', '').replace('-', '').isdigit():
                try:
                    data[key] = Decimal(value)
                except:
                    pass
        return data
    
    def write(self, filename: str, data: Any) -> bool:
        """Write record to self.json file - self.real implementation."""
        filepath = os.path.join(self.base_path, filename)
        # Convert Decimal to string for self.json serialization
        serializable = {}
        for key, value in (data.items() if isinstance(data, dict) else [("data", data)]):
            if isinstance(value, Decimal):
                serializable[key] = str(value)
            else:
                serializable[key] = value
        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(serializable, f, indent=2, default=str)
        return True

            self.ws_calc_principal = self.ws_calc_payment - self.ws_calc_interest
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_3210_calculate_payment: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_10210_score_payment_history(self) -> None:
        """Score payment history."""
        try:
            """10210-self.score-self.payment-self.history."""
            self.ws_payment_score = (self.ws_on_time_payments * 100) / (self.ws_on_time_payments + self.ws_late_30_days + self.ws_late_60_days + self.ws_late_90_days)

    def p_10500_generate_loan_terms(self) -> None:
        """Generate loan terms."""
        try:
            """10500-self.generate-self.loan-self.terms."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_loan_interest_rate = self.ws_approved_rate
            self.ws_monthly_rate = self.ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
            self.ws_compound_factor = (1 + self.ws_monthly_rate) ** self.ws_loan_term_months
            self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt = self.ws_loan_amount * self.ws_monthly_rate * self.ws_compound_factor / (self.ws_compound_factor - 1)
            self.ws_loan_principal_bal = self.ws_loan_amount
        except Exception as e:

            self.error_count += 1

    def p_10650_calculate_payment_split(self) -> None:
        """Calculate payment split."""
        try:
            """10650-self.calculate-self.payment-self.split."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.amort_interest[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_running_balance * self.ws_monthly_rate
            self.amort_principal[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt - self.amort_interest[self.ws_amort_idx]
            self.ws_running_balance -= self.amort_principal[self.ws_amort_idx]
            self.amort_balance[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_running_balance
            self.amort_payment_num[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_amort_idx
            self.amort_payment_amt[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt
            self.amort_escrow[self.ws_amort_idx] = (self.ws_property_tax + self.ws_insurance_premium) / 12
            self.amort_total_pmt[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt + self.amort_escrow[self.ws_amort_idx] + self.ws_pmi_amount
            self.amort_total_pmt[self.ws_amort_idx] = self.ws_loan_monthly_pmt
            self.p_10660_advance_payment_date()
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_10650_calculate_payment_split: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

            self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx] - self.ws_hold_cost
            self.hold_gain_loss[self.ws_hold_idx] / self.ws_hold_cost
            self.hold_pct_change[self.ws_hold_idx] = 0
            self.ws_total_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx]

    def p_11410_calculate_current_allocation(self) -> None:
        """Calculate current allocation."""
        try:
            """11410-self.calculate-self.current-self.allocation."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_stocks_value = 0
            self.ws_bonds_value = 0
            self.ws_cash_value = 0
            self.ws_hold_idx = 1
            self.ws_stocks_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx]
            self.ws_bonds_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx]
            self.ws_cash_value += self.hold_market_value[self.ws_hold_idx]
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1
            self.ws_stocks_pct = (self.ws_stocks_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
            self.ws_bonds_pct = (self.ws_bonds_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
            self.ws_cash_pct = (self.ws_cash_value / self.ws_total_value) * 100
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_11410_calculate_current_allocation: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

            self.ws_trade_status = 'self.open'

    def p_12510_calculate_costs(self) -> None:
        """Calculate costs."""
        try:
            """12510-self.calculate-self.costs."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_gross_amount = self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_executed_price
            self.ws_commission = self.ws_gross_amount * 0.0005
            self.ws_commission = self.ws_gross_amount * 0.001
            self.ws_commission = 4.95
            self.ws_fees = self.ws_gross_amount * 0.00002
            self.ws_net_amount = self.ws_gross_amount + self.ws_commission + self.ws_fees
            self.ws_net_amount = self.ws_gross_amount - self.ws_commission - self.ws_fees
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_12510_calculate_costs: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_12520_update_positions(self) -> None:

    def p_12530_update_cash(self) -> None:
        """Update cash."""

            """12530-self.update-self.cash."""

            self.ws_available_cash -= self.ws_net_amount

    def p_12525_add_to_position(self) -> None:
        """Add to position."""
        try:
            """12525-self.add-TO-self.position."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_hold_idx = 1
            self.ws_new_total_shares = self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] + self.ws_trade_shares
            self.ws_new_cost = (self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] * self.hold_cost_per_share[self.ws_hold_idx - 1]) + (self.ws_trade_shares * self.ws_executed_price)
            self.hold_cost_per_share[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = self.ws_new_cost / self.ws_new_total_shares
            self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] = self.ws_new_total_shares
            self.p_12527_create_new_position()
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_12525_add_to_position: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_12526_reduce_position(self) -> None:
        """Reduce position."""
        try:
            """12526-self.reduce-self.position."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_hold_idx = 1
            self.hold_shares[self.ws_hold_idx - 1] -= self.ws_trade_shares
            self.ws_realized_gain = self.ws_trade_shares * (self.ws_executed_price - self.hold_cost_per_share[self.ws_hold_idx - 1])
            self.ws_realized_gain_ytd += self.ws_realized_gain
            self.ws_hold_idx += 1
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_12526_reduce_position: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

            self.hold_shares[self.ws_holdings_count - 1] = self.ws_trade_shares
            self.hold_cost_per_share[self.ws_holdings_count - 1] = self.ws_executed_price
            self.hold_current_price[self.ws_holdings_count - 1] = self.ws_executed_price
            self.hold_purchase_date[self.ws_holdings_count - 1] = datetime.now()
        except Exception as e:

            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_14340_calc_fica(self) -> None:
        """Calc fica."""
        try:
            """14340-self.calc-self.fica."""

            self.ws_remaining_cap = 0
            self.ws_remaining_cap = 160200 - self.ws_ytd_gross
            self.ws_fica_ss = self.ws_gross_pay * 0.062

            self.ws_fica_medicare = self.ws_gross_pay * 0.0145
            self.ws_additional_medicare = 0
            self.ws_additional_medicare = self.ws_gross_pay * 0.009

    def p_14210_calc_salary_pay(self) -> None:
        """Calc salary pay."""
        try:

    def p_14220_calc_hourly_pay(self) -> None:
        """Calc hourly pay."""
        try:
            """14220-self.calc-self.hourly-self.pay."""
            self.ws_regular_pay = self.ws_hours_worked * self.ws_hourly_rate
            self.ws_overtime_pay = 0
            self.ws_regular_pay = 40 * self.ws_hourly_rate
            self.ws_ot_hours = self.ws_hours_worked - 40
            self.ws_overtime_pay = self.ws_ot_hours * self.ws_hourly_rate * 1.5
            self.ws_gross_pay = self.ws_regular_pay + self.ws_overtime_pay
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_14220_calc_hourly_pay: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_14230_calc_commission_pay(self) -> None:
        """Calc commission pay."""
        try:
            """14230-self.calc-self.commission-self.pay."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_base_pay = self.ws_base_salary / self.ws_pay_periods
            self.ws_commission_pay = self.ws_sales_amount * self.ws_commission_rate
            self.ws_gross_pay = self.ws_base_pay + self.ws_commission_pay
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_14230_calc_commission_pay: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_14310_calc_federal_tax(self) -> None:
        """Calc federal tax."""
        try:
            """14310-self.calc-self.federal-self.tax."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_annualized_gross = self.ws_gross_pay * self.ws_pay_periods
            self.ws_allowance_amount = self.ws_exemptions * 4300
            self.ws_taxable_income = self.ws_annualized_gross - self.ws_allowance_amount
            self.ws_taxable_income = 0
            self.p_14315_apply_tax_brackets()
            self.ws_federal_tax = self.ws_annual_tax / self.ws_pay_periods
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_14310_calc_federal_tax: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_14316_single_brackets(self) -> None:
        """Single brackets."""
        try:
            """14316-self.single-self.brackets."""
            self.ws_annual_tax = self.ws_taxable_income * 0.10
            self.ws_annual_tax = 1027.50 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 10275) * 0.12
            self.ws_annual_tax = 4807.50 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 41775) * 0.22
            self.ws_annual_tax = 15213.50 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 89075) * 0.24
            self.ws_annual_tax = 34647.50 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 170050) * 0.32
            self.ws_annual_tax = 49335.50 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 215950) * 0.35
            self.ws_annual_tax = 162718.00 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 539900) * 0.37
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_14316_single_brackets: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_14317_married_brackets(self) -> None:
        """Married brackets."""

            """14317-self.married-self.brackets."""

            self.ws_annual_tax = 9615.00 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 83550) * 0.22
            self.ws_annual_tax = 30427.00 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 178150) * 0.24
            self.ws_annual_tax = 69295.00 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 340100) * 0.32
            self.ws_annual_tax = 98671.00 + (self.ws_taxable_income - 431900) * 0.35

            self.ws_card_seq = random.random() * 999999999
            self.ws_card_number_temp = f'{self.ws_card_prefix}{self.ws_card_bin}{self.ws_card_seq}'
            self.p_23115_calculate_luhn_check()
            self.ws_card_number = f'{self.ws_card_number_temp}{self.ws_luhn_check}'
        except Exception as e:

    def p_23115_calculate_luhn_check(self) -> None:
        """Calculate luhn check."""
        try:
            """23115-self.calculate-self.luhn-self.check."""
            self.ws_luhn_sum = 0
            ws_luhn_idx = 15
            self.ws_luhn_digit = self.ws_card_number_temp[ws_luhn_idx-1]
            self.ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            self.ws_luhn_digit -= 9
            self.ws_luhn_sum += self.ws_luhn_digit
            self.ws_luhn_check = (10 - (self.ws_luhn_sum % 10)) % 10
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_23115_calculate_luhn_check: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_31320_calculate_fees(self) -> None:

            """31320-self.calculate-self.fees."""
            # v8.0: Business validation
            assert self.data is not None, "Data not initialized"
            self.ws_interchange_fee = self.ws_batch_total * 0.0175
            self.ws_assessment_fee = self.ws_batch_total * 0.0015
            self.ws_processor_fee = self.ws_batch_count * 0.10
            self.ws_total_fees = self.ws_interchange_fee + self.ws_assessment_fee + self.ws_processor_fee

    def p_99510_left_trim(self) -> None:
        """Left trim."""
        try:
            """99510-self.left-self.trim."""
            self.ws_lead_spaces = len(self.ws_input_string) - len(self.ws_input_string.lstrip())
            self.ws_output_string = self.ws_input_string[self.ws_lead_spaces:]
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_99510_left_trim: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

    def p_99520_right_trim(self) -> None:
        """Right trim."""
        try:
            """99520-self.right-self.trim."""
            self.ws_string_len = len(self.ws_input_string)
            self.ws_trail_spaces = len(self.ws_input_string) - len(self.ws_input_string.rstrip())
            self.ws_actual_len = self.ws_string_len - self.ws_trail_spaces
            self.ws_output_string = self.ws_input_string[:self.ws_actual_len]
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Error in p_99520_right_trim: {e}")
            self.error_count += 1
            raise

        """Calculate compound interest."""

            self.ws_compound_result = self.ws_principal * ((1 + self.ws_rate / self.ws_compounds_per_year) ** (self.ws_compounds_per_year * self.ws_years))
        except Exception as e:

            self.ws_total_inflows = 0
            self.ws_retail_outflow = self.ws_stable_deposits * 0.03 + self.ws_less_stable_deposits * 0.10
            self.ws_wholesale_outflow = self.ws_operational_deposits * 0.25 + self.ws_non_operational * 0.40
            self.ws_total_outflows += self.ws_retail_outflow
            self.ws_total_outflows += self.ws_wholesale_outflow
            self.ws_lcr_denominator = self.ws_total_outflows - min(self.ws_total_inflows, self.ws_total_outflows * 0.75)

            self.ws_market_losses = self.ws_trading_assets * self.ws_rate_shock / 100
            self.ws_stress_losses = self.ws_credit_losses + self.ws_market_losses

            self.ws_stressed_ratio = (self.ws_stressed_capital / self.ws_risk_weighted_assets) * 100
            self.ws_stress_pass_fail = 'self.pass_val'
            self.ws_stress_pass_fail = 'self.fail'

            self.error_count += 1
